LEFT JOIN "Skills" AS "s" ON "a"."Id" = "s"."IdAdminUser"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "e"."Id", "e"."Company", "e"."Description", "e"."EndDate", "e"."IdAdminUser", "e"."ImageUrl", "e"."Position", "e"."StartDate", "p"."Id" AS "Id0", "p"."Description" AS "Description0", "p"."IdExperience", "p"."ImageUrl" AS "ImageUrl0", "p"."Title", "p"."Url"

    SELECT "e"."Id", "e"."Company", "e"."Description", "e"."EndDate", "e"."IdAdminUser", "e"."ImageUrl", "e"."Position", "e"."StartDate", "p"."Id" AS "Id0", "p"."Description" AS "Description0", "p"."IdExperience", "p"."ImageUrl" AS "ImageUrl0", "p"."Title", "p"."Url"

SELECT "a"."Id", "a"."Description", "a"."Password", "a"."UserName", "a"."Email", "s"."Id", "s"."Category", "s"."IdAdminUser", "s"."Level", "s"."Name", "s0"."Id", "s0"."Company", "s0"."Description", "s0"."EndDate", "s0"."IdAdminUser", "s0"."ImageUrl", "s0"."Position", "s0"."StartDate", "s0"."Id0", "s0"."Description0", "s0"."IdExperience", "s0"."ImageUrl0", "s0"."Title", "s0"."Url"
FROM "AdminUsers" AS "a"
LEFT JOIN "Skills" AS "s" ON "a"."Id" = "s"."IdAdminUser"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "e"."Id", "e"."Company", "e"."Description", "e"."EndDate", "e"."IdAdminUser", "e"."ImageUrl", "e"."Position", "e"."StartDate", "p"."Id" AS "Id0", "p"."Description" AS "Description0", "p"."IdExperience", "p"."ImageUrl" AS "ImageUrl0", "p"."Title", "p"."Url"
    FROM "Experiences" AS "e"
    LEFT JOIN "Projects" AS "p" ON "e"."Id" = "p"."IdExperience"
) AS "s0" ON "a"."Id" = "s0"."IdAdminUser"
ORDER BY "a"."Id", "s"."Id", "s0"."Id"

FROM "AdminUsers" AS "a"
LEFT JOIN "Skills" AS "s" ON "a"."Id" = "s"."IdAdminUser"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "e"."Id", "e"."Company", "e"."Description", "e"."EndDate", "e"."IdAdminUser", "e"."ImageUrl", "e"."Position", "e"."StartDate", "p"."Id" AS "Id0", "p"."Description" AS "Description0", "p"."IdExperience", "p"."ImageUrl" AS "ImageUrl0", "p"."Title", "p"."Url"
    FROM "Experiences" AS "e"
    LEFT JOIN "Projects" AS "p" ON "e"."Id" = "p"."IdExperience"
) AS "s0" ON "a"."Id" = "s0"."IdAdminUser"
ORDER BY "a"."Id", "s"."Id", "s0"."Id"

FROM "AdminUsers" AS "a"
LEFT JOIN "Skills" AS "s" ON "a"."Id" = "s"."IdAdminUser"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "e"."Id", "e"."Company", "e"."Description", "e"."EndDate", "e"."IdAdminUser", "e"."ImageUrl", "e"."Position", "e"."StartDate", "p"."Id" AS "Id0", "p"."Description" AS "Description0", "p"."IdExperience", "p"."ImageUrl" AS "ImageUrl0", "p"."Title", "p"."Url"
    FROM "Experiences" AS "e"
    LEFT JOIN "Projects" AS "p" ON "e"."Id" = "p"."IdExperience"
) AS "s0" ON "a"."Id" = "s0"."IdAdminUser"
ORDER BY "a"."Id", "s"."Id", "s0"."Id"
